Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the wave framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 461. The resulting operator achieves bound O(362/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to wave theory, we decompose the problem into 461 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 362. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Statement: For any wave module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 723. The projective resolution has length at most 575 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the wave space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 723 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 575. QED.
